**Validate with:**

```bash
# Preferred front door
nix develop .#systems
nix build .#checks.x86_64-linux.systems-light   # use your host system, not bare checks.systems-light

# Full product scoreboard (needs stage1 lean + freestanding product bundle)
export PATH="$PWD/build/release/stage1/bin:${PATH:-}"
export LD_LIBRARY_PATH="${LD_LIBRARY_PATH:-/usr/lib:/lib:/usr/lib/x86_64-linux-gnu}"
./script/systems-validate.sh   # SCORE pass=N fail=0; PRODUCT_GC_FREE=1; GC_FREE_ELABORATOR honest
./script/systems-status.sh
# Slake classic CLI (not part of SCORE):
#   tests/slake/parity/run_parity.sh
#   SLAKE_DEPGRAPH=1 ./tests/slake/depgraph_cli_smoke.sh
# SCORE green alone does not prove DepGraph CLI / parity wiring.
```

## Strategic bottlenecks

| Bottleneck | Why it blocks parallel work | Unblock |
|------------|-----------------------------|---------|
| Thin product library | Few residual-clean modules for CompCert/embed stories | Continue PRODUCT_FS_NEXT waves (W65+) |
| Host elaborator still RC | Blocks “self-host without runtime” honesty | H5 earn (H1–H4 plan + H5 path/worklist/baseline snapshot/W0 verified docs/W1 experiment + candidate protocols/H5.E1 CMake re-verify/H5.E3 next experiment step/H5.E4 negative candidates/earn checklist `HOST_ELABORATOR_EARN_CHECKLIST=1`/FORCE refusal `HOST_ELABORATOR_FORCE_REFUSAL=1`/FORCE negative measured `HOST_ELABORATOR_FORCE_NEGATIVE_MEASURED=1`/status-quo summary `HOST_ELABORATOR_STATUS_QUO=1`/docs ladder complete `HOST_ELABORATOR_DOCS_LADDER_COMPLETE=1` docs done; stage1 still classic RC baseline; never forge =1; only real residual_free ELF remains; merge track with W107+) |
| Bash gate pile | Outsiders avoid tooling | Prefer systems.nix; T4 later |
| Concurrent assumed not proved | Parallelism story incomplete | After thicker linear surface |

---

## Do not regress

- Prefer extending **`systems.nix`** over new `script/systems-*.sh` gates  
- Never forge **`GC_FREE_ELABORATOR=1`**  
- Keep **product residual** independent of host residual  
- Light check stays pure-sandbox (`patchShebangs`); dual-path SKIP when bundle not in flake `self`  
- Stage flake-needed files so dirty `self` includes them before `nix build .#checks…`  
- Classic **`ci.yml` / master** defaults untouched  
- Product brand is **Systems** / **Parallelism** — do not reintroduce `Freestanding`/`Par` as public module roots  
- Do **not** reintroduce `PAR_*` / `PRODUCT_PAR_*` scoreboard aliases (primary `PARALLELISM_*` only)  
- `compiler.freestanding` remains the technical compile mode (industry term)
